# ZMem Launch Readiness

Current launch stance: local alpha is dogfood-ready; the public `zerkerlabs/zmem` repo exists and the Vercel production deployment for `zmem.sh` is live under Zerker Labs, but public alpha publish is still blocked until the clean-shell proof is captured, the launch assets are captured, the return packet is finalized, and the first alpha tag is cut.

For the compact execution checklist, use [ZMEM_LAUNCH_LIST.md](ZMEM_LAUNCH_LIST.md).

## Product Positioning

Launch as:

> Local-first verifiable memory for AI agents.

Public promise:

> ZMem gives every memory provenance, policy, receipts, and a trust gate before it influences action.

Keep the homepage memory-first. Use "context" only for the temporary task input an agent receives after policy-gated memory injection.

## Repo Plan

Chosen public repo:

```text
zerkerlabs/zmem
```

Why:

- Matches the public product/domain name.
- Matches the raw installer contract already documented in release proof.
- Keeps the repo and domain aligned while preserving the Python package name `zerker-memory`.
- Keeps the local git remote aligned with the launch contract.

Current local finding:

```text
origin   https://github.com/zerkerlabs/zmem.git
```

Current repo status: the local remote still targets `https://github.com/zerkerlabs/zmem.git`, and prior launch work recorded that the public repo exists with committed `main` pushed. In the current shell, `gh auth status` fails because the active `rezker1` token is invalid, so this run could not re-verify or advance the public push path directly. Important scope note: the pushed public repo currently contains the last committed `main`; the latest local swarm work still needs review and consolidation before it is pushed.

## Site Plan

Recommended day-1 site:

```text
https://zmem.sh
```

Use `zmem.sh` as the public launch domain now. It is short, CLI-shaped, and pairs well with `treeship.dev` as the proof layer.

Recommended path:

1. Ship `zmem.sh` as the public site.
2. Host the code under `zerkerlabs/zmem`.
3. Keep `zerker.ai/memory` or `memory.zerker.ai` as optional redirects from the broader Zerker surface.
4. Use `treeship.dev` as a visible proof companion, not a prerequisite the user must understand before installing ZMem.

## What Is Ready

From `zmem status --summary-only`:

- Workspace ready: yes.
- Doctor: ok.
- Memory proof ready: yes.
- Release packet ready: yes.
- Strict publish ready: no.
- Manual MCP pack ready: yes.
- Local alpha gate: ok with warnings.

Built product surface:

- Local SQLite memory.
- Typed memories and review queue.
- Policy-gated injection.
- Provenance and authority controls.
- Receipts, `why`, verify, bundles, snapshots, handoff/restore.
- Event Merkle root and selected-memory Merkle trees.
- MCP server and agent setup packs.
- Local dashboard.
- Landing page.
- Release proof and public verify packet machinery.

## Public Publish Blockers

Strict publish remains blocked on:

```text
launch_assets
public_verify_evidence
```

Required evidence:

- Clean-shell public verify logs under `.zerker/launch-proof/public-verify-logs/`.
- `.zerker/launch-proof/public-verify-result.json`.
- `.zerker/launch-proof/public-verify-summary.md`.
- Eight launch screenshots/GIFs under `.zerker/launch-proof/assets/`.
- Final verified return packet at `.zerker/launch-proof/public-verify-return-packet.tar.gz`.

Current site deployment:

- Vercel project: `zerkerlabs/zmem-site`.
- Production deployment: `https://zmem-site-fxsuil1pd-zerkerlabs.vercel.app`.
- Deployment id: `dpl_CcKdFKZ6qjNPvgnVUXY7ubmatavP`.
- GitHub integration: linked to `zerkerlabs/zmem`, production branch `main`, with Vercel project settings pinned to Root Directory `site`, Framework `Vite`, Install Command `npm ci`, Build Command `npm run build`, and Output Directory `dist`.
- Custom domains: `https://zmem.sh` redirects to `https://www.zmem.sh/`, and `https://www.zmem.sh` serves `HTTP/2 200` from Vercel.
- Correction note: the domain was first attached to the wrong Treeship scope, then moved to `zerkerlabs` with `vercel domains move zmem.sh zerkerlabs --scope treeship --yes` and aliased to the Zerker Labs deployment.

Remaining external completion steps after strict publish goes green:

- Cut `v0.1.0-alpha`.
